Mandatory before every checkpoint. ([gruggiero/typesafe4s](https://github.com/gruggiero/typesafe4s/tree/HEAD/openspec/schemas/verified-scala3/skills/openspec-adversarial-review) · ★4)

- **openspec-explore**: Chế độ Explore - người bạn đồng hành trong việc khám phá ý tưởng, điều tra vấn đề và làm rõ các yêu cầu. Sử dụng khi người dùng muốn suy nghĩ thấu đáo về một vấn đề trước hoặc trong quá trình thực hiện một thay đổi. ([hongquandev/OpenSpec-VI](https://github.com/hongquandev/OpenSpec-VI/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/openspec-explore))
- **openspec-explore**: 進入 explore 模式——在動手前或進行中，當使用者想先把想法、問題、需求想清楚時的思考夥伴。只思考不實作，可讀碼可查 codebase 可產 OpenSpec artifact，但不寫應用程式碼。Use when the user wants to think through something before or during a change；觸發：「先想清楚」「探索這個想法」「這個需求怎麼設計」「explore mode」。 ([KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins](https://github.com/KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/frontend-vue/skills/openspec-explore))
- **openspec-explore**: 進入探索模式——作為思考夥伴，探索想法、調查問題、釐清需求。當使用者想在開始或進行變更前深入思考某個主題時使用。 ([langley228/sdd-board](https://github.com/langley228/sdd-board/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/openspec-explore))
- **openspec-explore**: Explore an idea, investigate a problem, or compare designs with OpenSpec before implementation. Use for OpenSpec explore or opsx-explore requests. - **openspec-explore**: 进入探索模式 - 在使用 OpenSpec 的项目中，作为探索想法、调查问题、澄清需求的思考伙伴。当用户想在 OpenSpec 变更之前或期间先思考一番时使用。也在用户说 "openspec explore" 或 "opsx explore" 时使用。 ([liao666brant/go-ask](https://github.com/liao666brant/go-ask/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/openspec-explore) · 2 copies)
- **openspec-explore**: 进入探索模式，作为思考伙伴一起梳理想法、调查问题并澄清需求。当用户想在变更前或变更过程中先把事情想清楚时使用。 ([lightwhite-top/autu-qutu](https://github.com/lightwhite-top/autu-qutu/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/openspec-explore))
